represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n    \n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n    \n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n        \n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n        \n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n            \n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            \n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// Find the closest unclustered point to the current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\n// Returns -1 if no such point exists\nint findClosestPoint(const std::vector<int>& cluster_members,\n                     const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                     const std::vector<bool>& in_cluster,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold,\n                     const int point_count) {\n    int closest_point = -1;\n    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\n    \n    // Try each unclustered point as a candidate\n    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {\n        // Skip if already clustered or already in this cluster\n        if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;\n        \n        // Calculate the maximum distance from candidate to all cluster members\n        double max_dist = 0.0;\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = cluster_members[i];\n            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);\n            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);\n        }\n        \n        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best\n        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {\n            min_diameter = max_dist;\n            closest_point = candidate;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return closest_point;\n}\n\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster\nint generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                              const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                              const double threshold,\n                              const int point_count,\n                              std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {\n    std::vector<bool> in_cluster(point_count, false);\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    \n    //            threshold, point_count);\n        \n        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added\n        \n        in_cluster[closest] = true;\n        members.push_back(closest);\n std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    \n    // Initialize unclustered indices\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n unclustered_indices[i];\n            if (clustered[seed]) continue;\n            \n            std::vector<int> candidate_members;\n            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, cluster.members = best_cluster_members;\n            clusters.push_back(cluster);\n            \n            // Mark all members as clustered\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-fable-5","id":"msg_011CegsWz8sCkCoEgqWsEr77","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_012P69rvWLipEFeepBRBpJiS","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/home/llmtest/evals/20260901-162328/qtclustering_claude-fable-5-cc-medium_cuda_r2/qtclustering/qtclustering.cpp","content":"// QT Clustering Benchmark - CUDA Parallel Version\n//\n// QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters\n// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point\n// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.\n//\n// Parallelization: in every round of the outer loop, a candidate cluster\n// must be grown greedily from each remaining unclustered seed. On the GPU\n// one thread block handles one seed, and the threads of the block\n// parallelize over the candidate points. Each block maintains, for every\n// candidate point, the running maximum distance to the current cluster\n// members, so one greedy growth step costs O(N / blockDim) instead of\n// O(N * |cluster|). The host then selects the seed that produced the\n// largest candidate cluster (lowest index wins ties, exactly as in the\n// sequential version) and re-runs a single block to record its members.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cfloat>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                      \\\n    do {                                                                      \\\n        cudaError_t err__ = (call);                                           \\\n        if (err__ != cudaSuccess) {                                           \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d\\n\",                       \\\n                    cudaGetErrorString(err__), __FILE__, __LINE__);           \\\n            exit(1);                                                          \\\n        }                                                                     \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n\n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n\n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n\n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n\n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n\n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n__device__ inline double distDev(double x1, double y1, double x2, double y2) {\n    const double dx = x1 - x2;\n    const double dy = y1 - y2;\n    return sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\nstatic const int BLOCK_SIZE = 256;\n\n// Grow one candidate cluster per thread block. Each block grid-strides over\n// the seeds; scratch holds one row of N running max-distances per block.\n// A candidate's scratch entry is DBL_MAX when it is excluded (already\n// clustered, or already a member of the growing cluster).\n//\n// When `members` is non-null (single-block relaunch for the winning seed),\n// the member indices are recorded in the order they were added.\n__global__ void candidateClusterKernel(const double* __restrict__ xs,\n                                       const double* __restrict__ ys,\n                                       const int N,\n                                       const int* __restrict__ seeds,\n                                       const int numSeeds,\n                                       const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,\n                                       const double threshold,\n                                       double* __restrict__ scratch,\n                                       int* __restrict__ cardinality,\n                                       int* __restrict__ members) {\n    __shared__ double sval[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ int sidx[BLOCK_SIZE];\n\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n    double* maxd = scratch + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * N;\n\n    for (int seedIdx = blockIdx.x; seedIdx < numSeeds; seedIdx += gridDim.x) {\n        const int seed = seeds[seedIdx];\n        const double sx = xs[seed];\n        const double sy = ys[seed];\n\n        // Initialize running max-distance of every candidate to the seed.\n        for (int c = tid; c < N; c += blockDim.x) {\n            maxd[c] = (clustered[c] || c == seed) ? DBL_MAX\n                                                  : distDev(xs[c], ys[c], sx, sy);\n        }\n        if (members && tid == 0) {\n            members[0] = seed;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        int count = 1;\n        while (count < N) {\n            // Find the candidate with the smallest running max-distance that\n            // stays below the threshold; ties go to the lowest index, exactly\n            // matching the sequential findClosestPoint.\n            double bestv = DBL_MAX;\n            int besti = -1;\n            for (int c = tid; c < N; c += blockDim.x) {\n                const double v = maxd[c];\n                if (v < threshold && v < bestv) {\n                    bestv = v;\n                    besti = c;\n                }\n            }\n            sval[tid] = bestv;\n            sidx[tid] = besti;\n            __syncthreads();\n\n            for (int s = blockDim.x / 2; s > 0; s >>= 1) {\n                if (tid < s) {\n                    const double ov = sval[tid + s];\n                    const int oi = sidx[tid + s];\n                    if (oi >= 0 && (sidx[tid] < 0 || ov < sval[tid] ||\n                                    (ov == sval[tid] && oi < sidx[tid]))) {\n                        sval[tid] = ov;\n                        sidx[tid] = oi;\n                    }\n                }\n                __syncthreads();\n            }\n            const int best = sidx[0];\n            __syncthreads();\n\n            if (best < 0) break;  // No more points can be added\n\n            if (members && tid == 0) {\n                members[count] = best;\n            }\n            count++;\n\n            // Fold the new member into every candidate's running max-distance.\n            const double bx = xs[best];\n            const double by = ys[best];\n            for (int c = tid; c < N; c += blockDim.x) {\n                const double v = maxd[c];\n                if (c == best) {\n                    maxd[c] = DBL_MAX;\n                } else if (v != DBL_MAX) {\n                    const double d = distDev(xs[c], ys[c], bx, by);\n                    maxd[c] = (d > v) ? d : v;\n                }\n            }\n            __syncthreads();\n        }\n\n        if (tid == 0) {\n            cardinality[seedIdx] = count;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm (GPU-accelerated)\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<unsigned char> clustered(N, 0);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n\n    // Initialize unclustered indices\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    }\n\n    // Upload point coordinates once (structure-of-arrays for coalescing).\n    std::vector<double> h_xs(N), h_ys(N);\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        h_xs[i] = points[i].x;\n        h_ys[i] = points[i].y;\n    }\n\n    const int maxBlocks = std::min(N, 4096);\n\n    double *d_xs, *d_ys, *d_scratch;\n    int *d_seeds, *d_cardinality, *d_members;\n    unsigned char* d_clustered;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_xs, N * sizeof(double)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_ys, N * sizeof(double)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_scratch,\n                          static_cast<size_t>(maxBlocks) * N * sizeof(double)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_seeds, N * sizeof(int)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_cardinality, N * sizeof(int)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_members, N * sizeof(int)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_clustered, N * sizeof(unsigned char)));\n\n On the GPU\n// one thread block handles one seed, and the threads of the block\n// parallelize over the candidate points. Each block maintains, for every\n// candidate point, the running maximum distance to the current cluster\n// members, so one greedy growth step costs O(N / blockDim) instead of\n// O(N * |cluster|). The host then selects the seed that produced the\n// largest candidate cluster (lowest index wins ties, exactly as in the\n// sequential version) and re-runs a single block to record its members.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cfloat>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                      \\\n    do {                                                                      \\\n        cudaError_t err__ = (call);                                           \\\n        if (err__ != cudaSuccess) {                                           \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d\\n\",                       \\\n                    cudaGetErrorString(err__), __FILE__, __LINE__);           \\\n            exit(1);                                                          \\\n        }                                                                     \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n\n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n\n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n\n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n\n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n\n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n__device__ inline double distDev(double x1, double y1, double x2, double y2) {\n    const double dx = x1 - x2;\n    const double dy = y1 - y2;\n    return sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\nstatic const int BLOCK_SIZE = 256;\n\n// Grow one candidate cluster per thread block. Each block grid-strides over\n// the seeds; scratch holds one row of N running max-distances per block.\n// A candidate's scratch entry is DBL_MAX when it is excluded (already\n// clustered, or already a member of the growing cluster).\n//\n// When `members` is non-null (single-block relaunch for the winning seed),\n// the member indices are recorded in the order they were added.\n__global__ void candidateClusterKernel(const double* __restrict__ xs,\n                                       const double* __restrict__ ys,\n                                       const int N,\n                                       const int* __restrict__ seeds,\n                                       const int numSeeds,\n                                       const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,\n                                       const double threshold,\n                                       double* __restrict__ scratch,\n                                       int* __restrict__ cardinality,\n                                       int* __restrict__ members) {\n    __shared__ double sval[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ int sidx[BLOCK_SIZE];\n\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n    double* maxd = scratch + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * N;\n\n    for (int seedIdx = blockIdx.x; seedIdx < numSeeds; seedIdx += gridDim.x) {\n        const int seed = seeds[seedIdx];\n        const double sx = xs[seed];\n        const double sy = ys[seed];\n\n        // Initialize running max-distance of every candidate to the seed.\n        for (int c = tid; c < N; c += blockDim.x) {\n            maxd[c] = (clustered[c] || c == seed) ? DBL_MAX\n                                                  : distDev(xs[c], ys[c], sx, sy);\n        }\n        if (members && tid == 0) {\n            members[0] = seed;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        int count = 1;\n        while (count < N) {\n            // Find the candidate with the smallest running max-distance that\n            // stays below the threshold; ties go to the lowest index, exactly\n            // matching the sequential findClosestPoint.\n            double bestv = DBL_MAX;\n            int besti = -1;\n            for (int c = tid; c < N; c += blockDim.x) {\n                const double v = maxd[c];\n                if (v < threshold && v < bestv) {\n                    bestv = v;\n                    besti = c;\n                }\n            }\n            sval[tid] = bestv;\n            sidx[tid] = besti;\n            __syncthreads();\n\n            for (int s = blockDim.x / 2; s > 0; s >>= 1) {\n                if (tid < s) {\n                    const double ov = sval[tid + s];\n                    const int oi = sidx[tid + s];\n                    if (oi >= 0 && (sidx[tid] < 0 || ov < sval[tid] ||\n                                    (ov == sval[tid] && oi < sidx[tid]))) {\n                        sval[tid] = ov;\n                        sidx[tid] = oi;\n                    }\n                }\n                __syncthreads();\n            }\n            const int best = sidx[0];\n            __syncthreads();\n\n            if (best < 0) break;  // No more points can be added\n\n            if (members && tid == 0) {\n                members[count] = best;\n            }\n            count++;\n\n            // Fold the new member into every candidate's running max-distance.\n            const double bx        maxd[c] = DBL_MAX;\n                } else if (v != DBL_MAX) {\n                    const double d = distDev(xs[c], ys[c], bx, by);\n                    maxd[c] = (d > v) ? d : v;\n                }\n            }\n            __syncthreads();\n        }\n\n        if (tid == 0) {\n            cardinality[seedIdx] = count;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm (GPU-accelerated)\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<unsigned char> clustered(N, 0);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n\n    // Initialize unclustered unclustered seed in parallel.\n        const int numBlocks = std::min(numSeeds, maxBlocks);\n        candidateClusterKernel<<<numBlocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(\n            d_xs, d_ys, N, d_seeds, numSeeds, d_clustered, threshold,\n            d_scratch, d_cardinality, nullptr);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(h_cardinality.data(), d_cardinality,\n                              numSeeds * sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n\n        // Pick the largest candidate cluster; first seed wins ties (the\n        // seeds are stored in ascending order, matching the single block to record\n            // its member list.\n            candidateClusterKernel<<<1, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(\n                d_xs, d_ys, N, d_seeds + best_pos, 1, d_clustered, threshold,\n                d_scratch, d_cardinality, d_members);\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n\n            Cluster cluster;\n            cluster.seed_point = unclustered_indices[best_pos];\n            cluster.members.resize(max_cardinality);\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(cluster.members.data(), d_members,\n                                  max_cardinality * sizeof(int),\n                                  cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n\n            // Mark all members as clustered\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n                clustered[cluster.members[i]] = cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n\n            clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));\n\n            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\n            unclustered_indices.erase(\n          );\n        } else {\n            // No more clusters can be formed\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_xs));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_ys));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_scratch));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_seeds));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_cardinality));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_members));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_clustered));\n\n    return clusters;\n}\n\n// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties\nbool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold) {\n    bool valid = true;\n\n    printf(\"Validating clusters:\\n\");\n\n    // Check each cluster\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < and iteratively adding the closest point\n// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n    \n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n    \n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n        \n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n        \n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n            \n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            \n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// Find the closest unclustered point to the current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\n// Returns -1 if no such point exists\nint findClosestPoint(const std::vector<int>& cluster_members,\n                     const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                     const std::vector<bool>& in_cluster,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold,\n                     const int point_count) {\n    int closest_point = -1;\n    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\n    \n    // Try each unclustered point as a candidate\n    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {\n        // Skip if already clustered or already in this cluster\n        if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;\n        \n        // Calculate the maximum distance from candidate to all cluster members\n        double max_dist = 0.0;\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = cluster_members[i];\n            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);\n            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);\n        }\n        \n        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best\n        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {\n            min_diameter = max_dist;\n            closest_point = candidate;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return closest_point;\n}\n\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster\nint generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                              const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                              const double threshold,\n                              const int point_count,\n                              std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {\n    std::vector<bool> in_cluster(point_count, false);\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    \n    // Add seed point\n    in_cluster[seed_point] = true;\n    members.push_back(seed_point);\n    \n    // Iteratively add closest points\n    while (static_cast<int>(members.size()) < point_count) {\n        // Find closest point to current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\n        const int closest = findClosestPoint(members, clustered, in_cluster, points, \n                                             threshold, point_count);\n        \n        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added\n        \n        in_cluster[closest] = true;\n        members.push_back(closest);\n    }\n    \n    // Copy members if requested\n    if (cluster_members) {\n        *cluster_members = members;\n    }\n    \n    return static_cast<int>(members.size());\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    \n    // Initialize unclustered indices\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    }\n    \n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\n        int best_seed = -1;\n        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;\n        \n        // Try each unclustered point as a seed\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {\n            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];\n            if (clustered[seed]) continue;\n            \n            std::vector<int> candidate_members;\n            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, \n                                                       threshold, N, \n                                                       &candidate_members);\n            \n            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {\n                max_cardinality = cardinality;\n                best_seed = seed;\n                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;\n            }\n        }\n        \n        // If we found a cluster, add it\n        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {\n            Cluster cluster;\n            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\n            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;\n            clusters.push_back(cluster);\n            \n            // Mark all members as clustered\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;\n            }\n            \n            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\n            unclustered_indices.erase(\n                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\n                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),\n                unclustered_indices.end()\n            );\n        } else {\n            // No more clusters can be formed\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return clusters;\n}\n\n// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties\nbool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold) {\n    bool valid = true;\n    \n    printf(\"Validating clusters:\\n\");\n    \n    // Check each cluster\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];\n        double max_diameter = 0.0;\n        \n        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], \n                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n        \n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n            printf(\"  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\\n\", \n                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);\n        }\n        \n        // Validate diameter is within threshold\n        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error\n            printf(\"ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\\n\", \n                   c, max_diameter, threshold);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Check for duplicate memberships\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];\n            if (membership[member] >= 0) {\n                printf(\"ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\\n\",\n                       member, membership[member], c);\n                valid = false;\n            }\n            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Count clustered points\n    int clustered_count = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {\n        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\\n\",\n           points.size(), clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);\n    \n    return valid;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of points (default: 1000)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -t <float>   Distance threshold for clustering (default: 2.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int num_points = 1000;\n    double threshold = 2.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            num_points = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-t\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            threshold = atof(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    if (num_points <= 0 || threshold <= 0.0) {\n        printf(\"Error: Invalid parameters (num_points=%d, threshold=%.2f)\\n\", \n               num_points, threshold);\n        return 1;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"QT Clustering Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Number of points: %d\\n\", num_points);\n    printf(\"Distance threshold: %.2f\\n\", threshold);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    \n    // Generate synthetic data\n    std::vector<Point> points(num_points);\n    generateSyntheticData(points, num_points);\n    \n    // Perform QT clustering\n    auto cluster_start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    \n    const std::vector<Cluster> clusters = cluster_end - cluster_start);\n    \n    printf(\"Clustering time: %ld ms\\n\", cluster_time.count());\n    printf(\"Clusters found: %zu\\n\", clusters.size());\n    \n    // Calculate statistics and Since a candidate's running max-distance only grows, points\n// already at or beyond the threshold can never join, so\n// count + #viable_candidates is an upper bound on the final cardinality.\n// When that bound falls strictly below the best cardinality seen so far,\n// this seed cannot win (nor tie an earlier seed at globalMax) and the block\n// abandons it, reporting the current count (a value < globalMax, so the\n// host's winner selection is unaffected).\n__global__ void candidateClusterKernel(const double* __restrict__ xs,\n                                       const double* __restrict__ ys,\n                                       const int N,\n                                       const int* __restrict__ seeds,\n                                       const int numSeeds,\n                                       const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,\n                                       const double threshold,\n                                       double* __restrict__ scratch,\n                                       int* __restrict__ cardinality,\n                                       int* __restrict__ members,\n                                       int* __restrict__ globalMax) {\n    __shared__ double sval[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ int sidx[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ int scnt[BLOCK_SIZE];","originalFile":"// QT Clustering Benchmark - CUDA Parallel Version\n//\n// QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters\n// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point\n// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.\n//\n// Parallelization: in every round of the outer loop, a candidate cluster\n// must be grown greedily from each remaining unclustered seed. On the GPU\n// one thread block handles one seed, and the threads of the block\n// parallelize over the candidate points. Each block maintains, for every\n// candidate point, the running maximum distance to the current cluster\n// members, so one greedy growth step costs O(N / blockDim) instead of\n// O(N * |cluster|). The host then selects the seed that produced the\n// largest candidate cluster (lowest index wins ties, exactly as in the\n// sequential version) and re-runs a single block to record its members.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cfloat>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                      \\\n    do {                                                                      \\\n        cudaError_t err__ = (call);                                           \\\n        if (err__ != cudaSuccess) {                                           \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d\\n\",                       \\\n                    cudaGetErrorString(err__), __FILE__, __LINE__);           \\\n            exit(1);                                                          \\\n        }                                                                     \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n\n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n\n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n\n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n\n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n\n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n__device__ inline double distDev(double x1, double y1, double x2, double y2) {\n    const double dx = x1 - x2;\n    const double dy = y1 - y2;\n    return sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\nstatic const int BLOCK_SIZE = 256;\n\n// Grow one candidate cluster per thread block. Each block grid-strides over\n// the seeds; scratch holds one row of N running max-distances per block.\n// A candidate's scratch entry is DBL_MAX when it is excluded (already\n// clustered, or already a member of the growing cluster).\n//\n// When `members` is non-null (single-block relaunch for the winning seed),\n// the member indices are recorded in the order they were added.\n__global__ void candidateClusterKernel(const double* __restrict__ xs,\n                                       const double* __restrict__ ys,\n                                       const int N,\n                                       const int* __restrict__ seeds,\n                                       const int numSeeds,\n                                       const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,\n                                       const double threshold,\n                                       double* __restrict__ scratch,\n                                       int* __restrict__ cardinality,\n                                       int* __restrict__ members) {\n    __shared__ double sval[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ int sidx[BLOCK_SIZE];\n\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n    double* maxd = scratch + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * N;\n\n    for (int seedIdx = blockIdx.x; seedIdx < numSeeds; seedIdx += gridDim.x) {\n        const int seed = seeds[seedIdx];\n        const double sx = xs[seed];\n        const double sy = ys[seed];\n\n        // Initialize running max-distance of every candidate to the seed.\n        for (int c = tid; c < N; c += blockDim.x) {\n            maxd[c] = (clustered[c] || c == seed) ? blockDim.x) {\n                const double v = maxd[c];\n                if (v < threshold && v < bestv) {\n                    bestv = v;\n                    besti = c;\n                }\n __syncthreads();\n            }\n            const int best = sidx[0];\n            __syncthreads();\n\n            if (best < 0) break;  // No more points can be added\n\n            if (members && clustering algorithm (GPU-accelerated)\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<unsigned char> clustered(N, 0);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n\n    // Initialize unclustered indices\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    }\n\n    // Upload point coordinates once (structure-of-arrays for coalescing).\n    std::vector<double> CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_cardinality, N * sizeof(int)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_members, N * sizeof(int)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_clustered, N * sizeof(unsigned char)));\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_xs, h_xs.data(), N * sizeof(double),\n                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_ys, h_ys.data(), N * sizeof(double),\n                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_clustered, clustered.data(),\n                          N * sizeof(unsigned char), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n\n    std::vector<int> unclustered seed in parallel.\n        const int numBlocks = std::min(numSeeds, maxBlocks);\n        candidateClusterKernel<<<numBlocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(\n            d_xs, d_ys, N, d_seeds, numSeeds, On the GPU\n// one thread block handles one seed, and the threads of the block\n// parallelize over the candidate points. Each block maintains, for every\n// candidate point, the running maximum distance to the current cluster\n// members, so one greedy growth step costs O(N / blockDim) instead of\n// O(N * |cluster|). The host then selects the seed that produced the\n// largest candidate cluster (lowest index wins ties, exactly as in the\n// sequential version) and re-runs a single block to record its members.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cfloat>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                      \\\n    do {                                                                      \\\n        cudaError_t err__ = (call);                                           \\\n        if (err__ != cudaSuccess) {                                           \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d\\n\",                       \\\n                    cudaGetErrorString(err__), __FILE__, __LINE__);           \\\n            exit(1);                                                          \\\n        }                                                                     \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n\n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n\n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n\n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n\n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n\n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n__device__ inline double distDev(double x1, double y1, double x2, double y2) {\n    const double dx = x1 - x2;\n    const double dy = y1 - y2;\n    return sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\nstatic const int BLOCK_SIZE = 256;\n\n// Grow one candidate cluster per thread block. Each block grid-strides over\n// the seeds; scratch holds one row of N running max-distances per block.\n// A candidate's scratch entry is DBL_MAX when it is excluded (already\n// clustered, or already a member of the growing cluster).\n//\n// When `members` is non-null (single-block relaunch for the winning seed),\n// the member indices are recorded in the order they were added.\n//\n// `globalMax` (when non-null) tracks the best completed cardinality across\n// all blocks. Since a candidate's running max-distance only grows, points\n// already at or beyond the threshold can never join, so\n// count + #viable_candidates is an upper bound on the final cardinality.\n// When that bound falls strictly below the best cardinality seen so far,\n// this seed cannot win (nor tie an earlier seed at globalMax) and the block\n// abandons it, reporting the current count (a value < globalMax, so the\n// host's winner selection is unaffected).\n__global__ void candidateClusterKernel(const double* __restrict__ xs,\n                                       const double* __restrict__ ys,\n                                       const int N,\n                                       const int* __restrict__ seeds,\n                                       const int numSeeds,\n                                       const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,\n                                       const double threshold,\n                                       double* __restrict__ scratch,\n                                       int* __restrict__ cardinality,\n                                       int* __restrict__ members,\n                                       int* __restrict__ globalMax) {\n    __shared__ double sval[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ int sidx[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ int scnt[BLOCK_SIZE];\n\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n    double* maxd = scratch + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * N;\n\n    for (int seedIdx = blockIdx.x; seedIdx < numSeeds; seedIdx += gridDim.x) {\n        const int seed = seeds[seedIdx];\n        const double sx = xs[seed];\n        const double sy = ys[seed];\n\n        // Initialize running max-distance of every candidate to the seed.\n        for (int c = tid; c < N; c += blockDim.x) {\n            maxd[c] = (clustered[c] || c == seed) ? blockDim.x) {\n                const double v = maxd[c];\n                if (v < threshold && v < bestv) {\n                    bestv = v;\n                    besti = c;\n                }\n clustering algorithm (GPU-accelerated)\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N =       cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_clustered, clustered.data(),\n                          N * sizeof(unsigned char), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n\n    std::vector<int> h_cardinality(N);\n\n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        const int numSeeds = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());\n\n On the GPU\n// one thread block handles one seed, and the threads of the block\n// parallelize over the candidate points. Each block maintains, for every\n// candidate point, the running maximum distance to the current cluster\n// members, so one greedy growth step costs O(N / blockDim) instead of\n// O(N * |cluster|). The host then selects the seed that produced the\n// largest candidate cluster (lowest index wins ties, exactly as in the\n// sequential version) and re-runs a single block to record its members.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cfloat>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                      \\\n    do {                                                                      \\\n        cudaError_t err__ = (call);                                           \\\n        if (err__ != cudaSuccess) {                                           \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d\\n\",                       \\\n                    cudaGetErrorString(err__), __FILE__, __LINE__);           \\\n            exit(1);                                                          \\\n        }                                                                     \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n\n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n\n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n\n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n\n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n\n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n__device__ inline double distDev(double x1, double y1, double x2, double y2) {\n    const double dx = x1 - x2;\n    const double dy = y1 - y2;\n    return sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\nstatic const int BLOCK_SIZE = 256;\n\n// Grow one candidate cluster per thread block. Each block grid-strides over\n// the seeds; scratch holds one row of N running max-distances per block.\n// A candidate's scratch entry is DBL_MAX when it is excluded (already\n// clustered, or already a member of the growing cluster).\n//\n// When `members` is non-null (single-block relaunch for the winning seed),\n// the member indices are recorded in the order they were added.\n//\n// `globalMax` (when non-null) tracks the best completed cardinality across\n// all blocks. Since a candidate's running max-distance only grows, points\n// already at or beyond the threshold can never join, so\n// count + #viable_candidates is an upper bound on the final cardinality.\n// When that bound falls strictly below the best cardinality seen so far,\n// this seed cannot win (nor tie an earlier seed at globalMax) and the block\n// abandons it, reporting the current count (a value < globalMax, so the\n// host's winner selection is unaffected).\n__global__ void candidateClusterKernel(const double* __restrict__ xs,\n                                       const double* __restrict__ ys,\n                                       const int N,\n                                       const int* __restrict__ seeds,\n                                       const int numSeeds,\n                                       const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,\n                                       const double threshold,\n                                       double* __restrict__ scratch,\n                                       int* __restrict__ cardinality,\n                                       int* __restrict__ members,\n                                       int* __restrict__ globalMax) {\n    __shared__ double sval[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ int sidx[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ int scnt[BLOCK_SIZE];\n\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n    double* maxd = scratch + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * N;\n\n    for (int seedIdx = blockIdx.x; seedIdx < numSeeds; seedIdx += gridDim.x) {\n        const int seed = seeds[seedIdx];\n        const double sx = xs[seed];\n        const double sy = ys[seed];\n\n        // Initialize running max-distance of every candidate to the seed.\n        for (int c = tid; c < N; c += blockDim.x) {\n            maxd[c] = (clustered[c] || c == seed) ? DBL_MAX\n                                                  : distDev(xs[c], ys[c], sx, sy);\n        }\n        if (members && tid == 0) {\n            members[0] = seed;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        int count = 1;\n        while (count < N) {\n            // Find the candidate with the smallest running max-distance that\n            // stays below the threshold; ties go to the lowest index, exactly\n            // matching the sequential findClosestPoint.\n            double bestv = DBL_MAX;\n            int besti = -1;\n            int viable = 0;\n            for (int c = tid; c < N; c += blockDim.x) {\n                const double v = maxd[c];\n                if (v < threshold) {\n                    viable++;\n                    if (v < bestv) {\n                        bestv = v;\n                        besti = c;\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n            sval[tid] = bestv;\n            sidx[tid] = besti;\n            scnt[tid] = viable;\n            __syncthreads();\n\n            for (int s = blockDim.x / 2; s > 0; s >>= 1) {\n                if (tid < s) {\n                    const double ov = sval[tid + s];\n                    const int oi = sidx[tid + s];\n                    if (oi >= 0 && (sidx[tid] < 0 || ov < sval[tid] ||\n                                    (ov == sval[tid] && oi < sidx[tid]))) {\n                        sval[tid] = ov;\n                        sidx[tid] = oi;\n                    }\n                    scnt[tid] += scnt[tid + s];\n                }\n                __syncthreads();\n            }\n            const int best = sidx[0];\n            const int totalViable = scnt[0];\n            __syncthreads();\n\n            if (best < 0) break;  // No more points can be added\n\n            // A stale globalMax read only makes the pruning less aggressive.\n            if (globalMax && count + totalViable < *globalMax) break;\n\n            if (members && tid == 0) {\n                members[count] = best;\n            }\n            count++;\n\n            // Fold the new member into every candidate's running max-distance.\n            const double bx = xs[best];\n            const double by = ys[best];\n            for (int c = tid; c < N; c += blockDim.x) {\n                const double v = maxd[c];\n                if (c == best) {\n                    maxd[c] = DBL_MAX;\n                } else if (v != DBL_MAX) {\n                    const double d = distDev(xs[c], ys[c], bx, by);\n                    maxd[c] = (d > v) ? d : v;\n                }\n            }\n            __syncthreads();\n        }\n\n        if (tid == 0) {\n            cardinality[seedIdx] = count;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm (GPU-accelerated)\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<unsigned char> clustered(N, 0);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n\n    // Initialize unclustered indices\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    }\n\n    // Upload point coordinates once (structure-of-arrays for coalescing).\n    std::vector<double> h_xs(N), h_ys(N);\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        h_xs[i] = points[i].x;\n        h_ys[i] = points[i].y;\n    }\n\n    const int maxBlocks = std::min(N, 4096);\n\n    double *d_xs, *d_ys, *d_scratch;\n    int *d_seeds, *d_cardinality, *d_members;\n    unsigned char* d_clustered;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_xs, N * sizeof(double)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_ys, N * sizeof(double)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_scratch,\n                          static_cast<size_t>(maxBlocks) * N * sizeof(double)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_seeds, N * sizeof(int)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_cardinality, N * sizeof(int)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_members, N * sizeof(int)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_clustered, N * sizeof(unsigned char)));\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_xs, h_xs.data(), N * sizeof(double),\n                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_ys, h_ys.data(), N * sizeof(double),\n                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_clustered, clustered.data(),\n                          N * sizeof(unsigned char), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n\n    std::vector<int> h_cardinality(N);\n\n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        const int numSeeds = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());\n\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_seeds, unclustered_indices.data(),\n                              numSeeds * sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n\n        // Grow a candidate cluster from every unclustered seed in parallel.\n        const int numBlocks = std::min(numSeeds, maxBlocks);\n        candidateClusterKernel<<<numBlocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(\n            d_xs, d_ys, N, d_seeds, numSeeds, d_clustered, threshold,\n            d_scratch, d_cardinality, nullptr);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(h_cardinality.data(), d_cardinality,\n                              numSeeds * sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n\n        // Pick the largest candidate cluster; first seed wins ties (the\n        // seeds are stored in ascending order, matching the sequential scan).\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\n        int best_pos = -1;\n        for (int i = 0; i < numSeeds; ++i) {\n            if (h_cardinality[i] > max_cardinality) {\n                max_cardinality = h_cardinality[i];\n                best_pos = i;\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (best_pos >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {\n            // Re-grow the winning candidate with a single block to record\n            // its member list.\n            candidateClusterKernel<<<1, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(\n                d_xs, d_ys, N, d_seeds + best_pos, 1, d_clustered, threshold,\n                d_scratch, d_cardinality, d_members);\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n\n            Cluster cluster;\n            cluster.seed_point = unclustered_indices[best_pos];\n            cluster.members.resize(max_cardinality);\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(cluster.members.data(), d_members,\n                                  max_cardinality * sizeof(int),\n                                  cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n\n            // Mark all members as clustered\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n                clustered[cluster.members[i]] = 1;\n            }\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_clustered, clustered.data(),\n                                  N * sizeof(unsigned char),\n                                  cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n\n            clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));\n\n            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\n            unclustered_indices.erase(\n                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\n                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),\n                unclustered_indices.end()\n            );\n        } else {\n            // No more clusters can be formed\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_xs));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_ys));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_scratch));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_seeds));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_cardinality));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_members));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_clustered));\n\n    return clusters;\n}\n\n// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties\nbool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold) {\n    bool valid = true;\n\n    printf(\"Validating clusters:\\n\");\n\n    // Check each cluster\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];\n        double max_diameter = 0.0;\n\n        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]],\n                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n            printf(\"  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\\n\",\n                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);\n        }\n\n        // Validate diameter is within threshold\n        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error\n            printf(\"ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\\n\",\n                   c, max_diameter, threshold);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Check for duplicate memberships\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\n    for (size_t c = 0; c <        printf(\"ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\\n\",\n                       member, membership[member], c);\n                valid = false;\n            }\n
